Theorem 215.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: For any tissue module M satisfying the convergence analysis hypothesis, the class [M] in K_0(R) has order dividing 823. The projective resolution has length at most 92 with Betti numbers bounded by 3.

Proof: By the convergence analysis decomposition theorem, the relevant homology group H_823(S, ≠) vanishes for dimension 823. The extension on the ambient space provides the required map, and the obstruction class in H^92(S, F) is trivial. QED.
